    79	export async function createWorkbenchAgent(
    80	  options: CreateWorkbenchAgentOptions,
    81	): Promise<WorkbenchAgentBundle> {
    82	  const env = options.env ?? process.env
    83	  const profile = options.profile
    84	  const workspaceRoot =
    85	    options.workspaceRoot ?? resolveWorkspaceRoot(env, profile)
    86	  const tools = toolsForProfile(profile)
    87	
    88	  if (profile === 'office') {
    89	    // O2: create safe default root + first-run README when missing.
    90	    await ensureOfficeWorkspace(workspaceRoot)
    91	    // O3: seed bundled skills + conventional output dirs.
    92	    await ensureOfficeSkills(workspaceRoot)
    93	
    94	    // O5: long-run defaults (maxSteps / summarization / memory).
    95	    const defaults = await resolveOfficeRuntimeDefaults(profile, env, {
    96	      workspaceRoot,
    97	      maxStepsOverride: options.maxSteps,
    98	    })
    99	
   100	    // O4: optional docs + calendar MCP (env-gated; degrade on failure).
   101	    const mcp = await loadOfficeMcpTools(env)
   102	    const honestyTools = [...tools, ...mcp.toolNames]
   103	
   104	    const workspace = new Workspace({
   105	      id: 'workbench-office',
   106	      name: 'Workbench Office Workspace',
   107	      operationTimeoutMs: Number(env.WORKSPACE_OPERATION_TIMEOUT_MS ?? 30_000),
   108	      filesystem: {
   109	        backend: new NodeFilesystemBackend({
   110	          rootDir: workspaceRoot,
   111	          virtualMode: true,
   112	          contained: true,
   113	        }),
   114	      },
   115	      skills: {
   116	        rootPaths: [OFFICE_SKILLS_VIRTUAL_ROOT],
   117	        autoDiscover: true,
   118	      },
   119	      toolConfig: officeFilesystemToolConfig(),
   120	    })
   121	
   122	    const mcpInstruction =
   123	      mcp.toolNames.length > 0
   124	        ? [
   125	            'Optional MCP tools may be available for docs/knowledge and calendar (names as listed in tools).',
   126	            'Prefer read-only MCP tools; write/update/delete MCP tools require user approval.',
   127	            'If MCP is unavailable, continue with local Workspace FS and skills only — do not invent cloud content.',
   128	          ].join(' ')
   129	        : 'MCP docs/calendar connectors are not connected in this session; use local Workspace FS and skills only.'
   130	
   131	    const agent = new Agent({
   132	      id: 'workbench',
   133	      name: 'workbench',
   134	      purpose: '本机办公 Agent Runtime（Workspace FS + Skills + 可选 MCP · 非远程生产集群）',
   135	      instructions: [
   136	        'You are the local Office Agent Runtime for UI Lab Agent Workbench.',
   137	        'Respond in Chinese unless the user writes in another language.',
   138	        'Use Workspace filesystem tools (ls, read_file, write_file, edit_file, …) inside the authorized root.',
   139	        'Office skills live under /skills (meeting-notes, weekly-report, research-brief).',
   140	        'When a request matches a skill: workspace_list_skills or workspace_search_skills → workspace_activate_skill → workspace_read_skill → follow SKILL.md → write deliverable under the skill output path.',
   141	        'Deliverable paths: /output/meeting-notes/, /output/weekly-report/, /output/research-brief/.',
   142	        mcpInstruction,
   143	        'All file paths must be virtual workspace paths starting with / (e.g. /notes/a.md, /output/meeting-notes/notes.md).',
   144	        'Never use host absolute paths (/Users/..., /home/..., drive letters). Never paste operator host paths into tools.',
   145	        'Prefer planning briefly, then read before write. Writes and deletes require user approval.',
   146	        'Do not claim to be a remote multi-tenant production cluster — this is a local office sidecar.',
   147	      ].join(' '),
   148	      model: options.model,
   149	      workspace,
   150	      workspaceToolkits: {
   151	        filesystem: {},
   152	        sandbox: false,
   153	        search: false,
   154	        skills: {},
   155	      },
   156	      workspaceSkillsPrompt: {
   157	        includeAvailable: true,
   158	        includeActivated: true,
   159	        maxAvailable: 10,
   160	        maxActivated: 5,
   161	      },
   162	      // MCP tools only when connected — never invent unavailable cloud tools.
   163	      ...(mcp.tools.length > 0
   164	        ? { tools: mcp.tools as (Tool<any, any> | Toolkit)[] }
   165	        : {}),
   166	      maxSteps: defaults.maxSteps,
   167	      summarization: defaults.summarization,
   168	      // false = stateless; Memory instance = multi-turn (conversationId = taskId).
   169	      memory: defaults.memory,
   170	    })
   171	
   172	    return {
   173	      profile,
   174	      agent,
   175	      workspaceRoot,
   176	      tools: honestyTools,
   177	      workspace,
   178	      maxSteps: defaults.maxSteps,
   179	      summarizationEnabled: defaults.summarization !== false,
   180	      memoryKind: defaults.memoryKind,
   181	      mcpStatuses: mcp.statuses,
   182	      mcpStatusLine: formatMcpStatusLine(mcp.statuses),
   183	      disconnectMcp: mcp.disconnect,
   184	    }
   185	  }
   186	
   187	  // minimal — DIY tools (M1–M3 baseline)
   188	  const defaults = await resolveOfficeRuntimeDefaults(profile, env, {
   189	    workspaceRoot,
   190	    maxStepsOverride: options.maxSteps,
   191	  })
   192	  const agent = new Agent({
   193	    id: 'workbench',
   194	    name: 'workbench',
   195	    purpose: '本机最小 Agent Runtime（DIY 工具 · 非远程生产集群）',
   196	    instructions: [
   197	      'You are the local Agent Runtime for UI Lab Agent Workbench.',
   198	      'Respond in Chinese unless the user writes in another language.',
   199	      'You may use read_file, write_file (requires approval), and run_command tools when helpful.',
   200	      'Prefer concise answers. Stay within the workspace tools for file access.',
   201	      'This is a local demo sidecar, not a remote production cluster.',
   202	    ].join(' '),
   203	    model: options.model,
   204	    tools: workbenchTools as (Tool<any, any> | Toolkit)[],
   205	    maxSteps: defaults.maxSteps,
   206	  })
   207	
   208	  return {
   209	    profile,
   210	    agent,
   211	    workspaceRoot,
   212	    tools,
   213	    maxSteps: defaults.maxSteps,
   214	    summarizationEnabled: false,
   215	    memoryKind: defaults.memoryKind,
   216	    mcpStatuses: [],
   217	    mcpStatusLine: 'docs=off,calendar=off',
   218	    disconnectMcp: async () => {},
   219	  }
   220	}

    53	  )

    55	}
    56

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..0eac360

@@ -0,0 +1,327 @@
+/**
+ * Office Profile O4 — optional MCP connectors (docs knowledge + calendar).
+ *
+ * - Env-configured only; no MCP SDK in the browser Renderer
+ * - Missing config / connect failure → honest degrade (local FS still works)
+ * - Side-effect tools get needsApproval for HITL
+ *
+ * Spec: docs/plans/voltagent-office-profile-spec.md (O4)
+ */
+
+import { MCPConfiguration, type Tool } from '@voltagent/core'
+import type { ProfileEnv } from './profile.js'
+
+export type McpConnectorId = 'docs' | 'calendar'
+
+/**
+ * Subset of VoltAgent MCP server config we support (type not re-exported from core).
+ */
+export type OfficeMcpServerConfig =
+  | {
+      type: 'http'
+      url: string
+      requestInit?: RequestInit
+      timeout?: number
+    }
+  | {
+      type: 'stdio'
+      command: string
+      args?: string[]
+      cwd?: string
+      env?: Record<string, string>
+      timeout?: number
+    }
+
+export type McpConnectorStatus = {
+  id: McpConnectorId
+  /** disabled = no env; connected = tools loaded; failed = configured but error */
+  status: 'disabled' | 'connected' | 'failed'
+  reason?: string
+  toolNames: string[]
+  transport?: 'http' | 'stdio'
+}
+
+export type OfficeMcpLoadResult = {
+  tools: Tool<any, any>[]
+  toolNames: string[]
+  statuses: McpConnectorStatus[]
+  /** Call on process shutdown when stdio MCP was used. */
+  disconnect: () => Promise<void>
+}
+
+export type ResolvedMcpServer = {
+  id: McpConnectorId
+  server: OfficeMcpServerConfig
+  transport: 'http' | 'stdio'
+}
+
+type McpHost = {
+  getTools: (
+    servers: Record<string, OfficeMcpServerConfig>,
+  ) => Promise<{ tools: Tool<any, any>[]; disconnect: () => Promise<void> }>
+}
+
+/**
+ * Heuristic: write / mutate cloud docs or calendar need HITL approval.
+ * Read/list/search stay free.
+ */
+export function isSideEffectMcpToolName(name: string): boolean {
+  const n = name.toLowerCase()
+  // Read-like wins when both match (e.g. list_writable_calendars).
+  if (
+    /(^|[_-])(read|list|get|search|query|fetch|find|show|lookup|describe|stat)([_-]|$)/.test(
+      n,
+    )
+  ) {
+    return false
+  }
+  return /(^|[_-])(write|create|update|delete|remove|edit|patch|append|send|post|put|modify|insert|schedule|cancel_event|add_)([_-]|$)/.test(
+    n,
+  )
+}
+
+/** Attach needsApproval to side-effect MCP tools (mutates tool objects). */
+export function applyMcpNeedsApproval(
+  tools: Tool<any, any>[],
+): Tool<any, any>[] {
+  for (const tool of tools) {
+    if (isSideEffectMcpToolName(tool.name)) {
+      // VoltAgent Tool exposes needsApproval on the instance.
+      ;(tool as { needsApproval?: boolean }).needsApproval = true
+    }
+  }
+  return tools
+}
+
+function parseArgs(raw: string | undefined): string[] | undefined {
+  if (!raw?.trim()) return undefined
+  // Prefer JSON array; fall back to comma-separated.
+  const t = raw.trim()
+  if (t.startsWith('[')) {
+    try {
+      const parsed = JSON.parse(t) as unknown
+      if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.every((x) => typeof x === 'string')) {
+        return parsed
+      }
+    } catch {
+      // fall through
+    }
+  }
+  return t.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
+}
+
+function bearerHeaders(env: ProfileEnv, prefix: string): RequestInit | undefined {
+  const token =
+    env[`${prefix}_BEARER_TOKEN`]?.trim() ||
+    env[`${prefix}_TOKEN`]?.trim() ||
+    env.MCP_BEARER_TOKEN?.trim()
+  if (!token) return undefined
+  return {
+    headers: {
+      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
+    },
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Resolve a single connector from env.
+ * URL → http transport; COMMAND → stdio. Empty → disabled.
+ */
+export function resolveMcpConnector(
+  id: McpConnectorId,
+  env: ProfileEnv = process.env,
+): ResolvedMcpServer | null {
+  const upper = id === 'docs' ? 'DOCS' : 'CALENDAR'
+  const url =
+    env[`MCP_${upper}_URL`]?.trim() ||
+    env[`FEISHU_${upper}_MCP_URL`]?.trim() ||
+    (id === 'docs'
+      ? env.FEISHU_DOCS_MCP_URL?.trim()
+      : env.FEISHU_CALENDAR_MCP_URL?.trim())
+
+  if (url) {
+    const requestInit = bearerHeaders(env, `MCP_${upper}`)
+    return {
+      id,
+      transport: 'http',
+      server: {
+        type: 'http',
+        url,
+        ...(requestInit ? { requestInit } : {}),
+        timeout: Number(env.MCP_TIMEOUT_MS ?? 20_000) || 20_000,
+      },
+    }
+  }
+
+  const command =
+    env[`MCP_${upper}_COMMAND`]?.trim() ||
+    env[`FEISHU_${upper}_MCP_COMMAND`]?.trim()
+  if (command) {
+    const args = parseArgs(env[`MCP_${upper}_ARGS`]) ?? []
+    return {
+      id,
+      transport: 'stdio',
+      server: {
+        type: 'stdio',
+        command,
+        args,
+        env: filterProcessEnvForChild(env),
+        timeout: Number(env.MCP_TIMEOUT_MS ?? 20_000) || 20_000,
+      },
+    }
+  }
+
+  return null
+}
+
+/** Do not leak the full process env into MCP child (avoid secrets scatter). */
+function filterProcessEnvForChild(
+  env: ProfileEnv,
+): Record<string, string> | undefined {
+  const allow = [
+    'PATH',
+    'HOME',
+    'LANG',
+    'LC_ALL',
+    'NODE_ENV',
+    // Feishu / calendar common keys if operator set them for the MCP server
+    'FEISHU_APP_ID',
+    'FEISHU_APP_SECRET',
+    'LARK_APP_ID',
+    'LARK_APP_SECRET',
+    'GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS',
+  ]
+  const out: Record<string, string> = {}
+  for (const key of allow) {
+    const v = env[key]
+    if (typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0) out[key] = v
+  }
+  // Explicit pass-through map: MCP_CHILD_ENV_KEYS=FOO,BAR
+  const extra = parseArgs(env.MCP_CHILD_ENV_KEYS) ?? []
+  for (const key of extra) {
+    const v = env[key]
+    if (typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0) out[key] = v
+  }
+  return Object.keys(out).length > 0 ? out : undefined
+}
+
+export function resolveAllMcpConnectors(
+  env: ProfileEnv = process.env,
+): ResolvedMcpServer[] {
+  const out: ResolvedMcpServer[] = []
+  for (const id of ['docs', 'calendar'] as const) {
+    const resolved = resolveMcpConnector(id, env)
+    if (resolved) out.push(resolved)
+  }
+  return out
+}
+
+async function defaultMcpHost(
+  servers: Record<string, OfficeMcpServerConfig>,
+): Promise<{ tools: Tool<any, any>[]; disconnect: () => Promise<void> }> {
+  const mcp = new MCPConfiguration({
+    // core types are wider; our subset is compatible at runtime
+    servers: servers as any,
+  })
+  try {
+    const tools = (await mcp.getTools()) as Tool<any, any>[]
+    return {
+      tools,
+      disconnect: async () => {
+        try {
+          await mcp.disconnect()
+        } catch {
+          // best-effort
+        }
+      },
+    }
+  } catch (err) {
+    try {
+      await mcp.disconnect()
+    } catch {
+      // ignore
+    }
+    throw err
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Load configured MCP tools. Never throws for missing config or connect failure.
+ */
+export async function loadOfficeMcpTools(
+  env: ProfileEnv = process.env,
+  options?: { host?: McpHost },
+): Promise<OfficeMcpLoadResult> {
+  const host = options?.host ?? { getTools: defaultMcpHost }
+  const resolved = resolveAllMcpConnectors(env)
+  const statuses: McpConnectorStatus[] = []
+  const disconnectors: Array<() => Promise<void>> = []
+  const allTools: Tool<any, any>[] = []
+  const allNames: string[] = []
+
+  for (const id of ['docs', 'calendar'] as const) {
+    const conf = resolved.find((r) => r.id === id)
+    if (!conf) {
+      statuses.push({
+        id,
+        status: 'disabled',
+        reason: '未配置 MCP_*_URL 或 MCP_*_COMMAND',
+        toolNames: [],
+      })
+      continue
+    }
+
+    try {
+      const { tools, disconnect } = await host.getTools({
+        [id]: conf.server,
+      })
+      const approved = applyMcpNeedsApproval(tools)
+      const names = approved.map((t) => t.name)
+      allTools.push(...approved)
+      allNames.push(...names)
+      disconnectors.push(disconnect)
+      statuses.push({
+        id,
+        status: 'connected',
+        toolNames: names,
+        transport: conf.transport,
+      })
+    } catch (err) {
+      const message =
+        err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err ?? 'unknown error')
+      statuses.push({
+        id,
+        status: 'failed',
+        reason: `MCP ${id} 连接失败：${message}`,
+        toolNames: [],
+        transport: conf.transport,
+      })
+    }
+  }
+
+  return {
+    tools: allTools,
+    toolNames: allNames,
+    statuses,
+    disconnect: async () => {
+      for (const d of disconnectors) {
+        await d()
+      }
+    },
+  }
+}
+
+/** Compact one-line status for sidecar logs. */
+export function formatMcpStatusLine(statuses: McpConnectorStatus[]): string {
+  return statuses
+    .map((s) => {
+      if (s.status === 'connected') {
+        return `${s.id}=ok(${s.toolNames.length})`
+      }
+      if (s.status === 'failed') {
+        return `${s.id}=fail`
+      }
+      return `${s.id}=off`
+    })
+    .join(',')
+}

    75	type UiToolPart = {
    76	  type: string
    77	  toolCallId: string
    78	  toolName: string

    85	  | string
    86	  | Array<{
    87	      id: string
    88	      role: 'user' | 'assistant'
    89	      parts: Array<{ type: string; text?: string } | UiToolPart>
    90	    }>

    92	/**
    93	 * Workspace tools use virtual paths (start with `/` under the authorized root).
    94	 * Models sometimes emit host absolute paths; map known shapes back to virtual
    95	 * so resume executes against the real workspace file.
    96	 */
    97	export function normalizeWorkspaceToolInput(input: unknown): unknown {
    98	  if (typeof input !== 'object' || input === null) return input
    99	  const rec = { ...(input as Record<string, unknown>) }
   100	  for (const key of ['file_path', 'path', 'filePath'] as const) {
   101	    const v = rec[key]
   102	    if (typeof v !== 'string' || v.length === 0) continue
   103	    // Already a short virtual path — keep.
   104	    if (/^\/(output|notes|skills)(\/|$)/i.test(v) && !v.includes('/Users/')) {
   105	      continue
   106	    }
   107	    // Host absolute path: take the last /output/ or /notes/ segment.
   108	    const idx = Math.max(v.lastIndexOf('/output/'), v.lastIndexOf('/notes/'))
   109	    if (idx >= 0) {
   110	      rec[key] = v.slice(idx)
   111	      continue
   112	    }
   113	    // Fallback: basename under virtual /output/
   114	    if (v.includes('/Users/') || v.includes('/home/') || /^[A-Za-z]:\\/.test(v)) {
   115	      const base = v.split(/[/\\]/).filter(Boolean).pop()
   116	      if (base) rec[key] = `/output/${base}`
   117	    }
   118	  }
   119	  return rec
   120	}
   121	
   122	/** Fallback tools when sidecar metadata cannot be loaded (minimal DIY). */
   123	const FALLBACK_TOOLS = ['read_file', 'write_file', 'run_command'] as const
   124	
   125	export class VoltAgentRuntimeAdapter implements RuntimePort {
   126	  private readonly baseUrl: string
   127	  private readonly agentId: string
   128	  private readonly projectId: string
   129	  private readonly schemaVersion: number
   130	  private readonly fetchImpl: typeof fetch
   131	  private readonly userId: string
   132	  private readonly nowIso: () => string
   133	  private readonly maxSteps: number | undefined
   134	  private readonly toolsOverride: string[] | undefined
   135	  private toolsCache: string[] | null = null
   136	
   137	  private readonly listeners = new Map<string, Set<Listener>>()
   138	  private readonly taskState = new Map<string, TaskStreamState>()
   139	  private seq = 0
   140	
   141	  constructor(options: VoltAgentRuntimeAdapterOptions) {
   142	    this.baseUrl = options.baseUrl.replace(/\/$/, '')
   143	    this.agentId = options.agentId
   144	    this.projectId = options.projectId
   145	    this.schemaVersion = options.schemaVersion ?? 1
   146	    this.fetchImpl = options.fetchImpl ?? fetch.bind(globalThis)
   147	    this.userId = options.userId ?? 'workbench-user'
   148	    this.nowIso = options.nowIso ?? (() => new Date().toISOString())
   149	    this.maxSteps = options.maxSteps
   150	    this.toolsOverride =
   151	      options.tools && options.tools.length > 0 ? [...options.tools] : undefined
   152	  }
   153	
   154	  subscribe(
   155	    taskId: string,
   156	    _cursor: number | string | null | undefined,
   157	    listener: Listener,
   158	  ): () => void {
   159	    let set = this.listeners.get(taskId)
   160	    if (!set) {
   161	      set = new Set()
   162	      this.listeners.set(taskId, set)
   163	    }
   164	    set.add(listener)
   165	    return () => {
   166	      set!.delete(listener)
   167	      if (set!.size === 0) this.listeners.delete(taskId)
   168	    }
   169	  }
   170	
   171	  async getSnapshot(taskId: string, runId?: string): Promise<RuntimeSnapshot | null> {
   172	    const state = this.taskState.get(taskId)
   173	    if (!state) return null
   174	    return {
   175	      taskId,
   176	      runId: runId ?? state.lastRunId ?? undefined,
   177	      protocolVersion: this.schemaVersion,
   178	      lastTaskSequence: Math.max(0, state.nextSequence - 1),
   179	    }
   180	  }
   181	
   182	  async getCapabilities(
   183	    _projectId: string,
   184	    _environmentId: string,
   185	  ): Promise<RuntimeCapabilities> {
   186	    const tools = await this.resolveActiveTools()
   187	    return {
   188	      projectId: this.projectId,
   189	      environmentId: 'local-voltagent',
   190	      features: {
   191	        steer: false,
   192	        queueFollowUp: false,
   193	        approval: true,
   194	        runInput: false,
   195	        cancel: true,
   196	      },
   197	      models: ['voltagent-sidecar'],
   198	      tools,
   199	    }
   200	  }
   201	
   202	  /** Prefer live sidecar agent tools; fall back to override / minimal DIY. */
   203	  private async resolveActiveTools(): Promise<string[]> {
   204	    if (this.toolsOverride) return [...this.toolsOverride]
   205	    if (this.toolsCache) return [...this.toolsCache]
   206	    try {
   207	      const res = await this.fetchImpl(
   208	        `${this.baseUrl}/agents/${encodeURIComponent(this.agentId)}`,
   209	        { method: 'GET' },
   210	      )
   211	      if (res.ok) {
   212	        const json = (await res.json()) as {
   213	          data?: { tools?: Array<{ name?: string }> }
   214	          tools?: Array<{ name?: string }>
   215	        }
   216	        const raw = json.data?.tools ?? json.tools ?? []
   217	        const names = raw
   218	          .map((t) => (typeof t?.name === 'string' ? t.name : ''))
   219	          .filter(Boolean)
   220	        if (names.length > 0) {
   221	          this.toolsCache = names
   222	          return [...names]
   223	        }
   224	      }
   225	    } catch {
   226	      // Sidecar down — honest minimal fallback.
   227	    }
   228	    return [...FALLBACK_TOOLS]
   229	  }
   230	
   231	  async startRun(
   232	    input: RunStartInput,
   233	    _idempotencyKey: string,
   234	  ): Promise<CommandAcknowledgement> {
   235	    this.ensureTask(input.taskId)
   236	    const state = this.taskState.get(input.taskId)!
   237	    state.lastRunId = input.proposedRunId
   238	    state.lastTurnId = input.turnId
   239	    return accepted(`start-${input.proposedRunId}`, this.nowIso())
   240	  }
   241	
   242	  async sendCommand(command: ApplicationCommand): Promise<CommandAcknowledgement> {
   243	    switch (command.type) {
   244	      case 'createTask':
   245	        this.ensureTask(command.proposedTaskId)
   246	        return accepted(command.commandId, this.nowIso())
   247	      case 'submitTurn':
   248	        return this.handleSubmitTurn(command)
   249	      case 'cancelRun':
   250	        return this.handleCancel(command.commandId, command.taskId)
   251	      case 'respondToApproval':
   252	        return this.handleApproval(command)
   253	      case 'retryTurn':
   254	        return rejected(
   255	          command.commandId,
   256	          'retry_via_submit',
   257	          '请通过新的 submitTurn 重试（VoltAgent Adapter 首版）',
   258	        )
   259	      case 'provideRunInput':
   260	      case 'queueFollowUp':
   261	      case 'steerRun':
   262	      case 'reconcileInterruptedRun':
   263	        return unsupported(
   264	          command.commandId,
   265	          `${command.type}_unsupported`,
   266	          `VoltAgent Adapter 暂不支持 ${command.type}`,
   267	        )
   268	      default:
   269	        return unsupported(
   270	          (command as ApplicationCommand).commandId,
   271	          'unknown_command',
   272	          '未知命令',
   273	        )
   274	    }
   275	  }
   276	
   277	  private ensureTask(taskId: string): TaskStreamState {
   278	    let state = this.taskState.get(taskId)
   279	    if (!state) {
   280	      state = {
   281	        nextSequence: 1,
   282	        activeAbort: null,
   283	        lastRunId: null,
   284	        lastTurnId: null,
   285	        lastUserText: null,
   286	        pendingApprovals: new Map(),
   287	      }
   288	      this.taskState.set(taskId, state)
   289	    }
   290	    return state
   291	  }
   292	
   293	  private emit(taskId: string, event: RuntimeSubscriptionEvent): void {
   294	    const set = this.listeners.get(taskId)
   295	    if (!set) return
   296	    for (const listener of set) listener(event)
   297	  }
   298	
   299	  private emitEnvelope(taskId: string, envelope: AgentRuntimeEventEnvelope): void {
   300	    this.emit(taskId, { kind: 'event', envelope })
   301	  }
   302	
   303	  private allocateIds(taskId: string, command: SubmitTurnCommand) {
   304	    this.seq += 1
   305	    const turnId =
   306	      command.proposedTurnId ?? command.turnId ?? `turn-${taskId}-${this.seq}`
   307	    const runId =
   308	      command.proposedRunId ?? command.runId ?? `run-${taskId}-${this.seq}`
   309	    return { turnId, runId }
   310	  }
   311	
   312	  private pushBookkeeping(
   313	    taskId: string,
   314	    turnId: string,
   315	    runId: string,
   316	    inputText: string,
   317	  ): void {
   318	    const state = this.ensureTask(taskId)
   319	    const push = (eventType: string, payload: unknown) => {
   320	      const occurredAt = this.nowIso()
   321	      const envelope: AgentRuntimeEventEnvelope = {
   322	        eventId: `va-book-${runId}-${state.nextSequence}`,
   323	        eventType,
   324	        schemaVersion: this.schemaVersion,
   325	        projectId: this.projectId,
   326	        taskId,
   327	        turnId,
   328	        runId,
   329	        taskSequence: state.nextSequence,
   330	        occurredAt,
   331	        receivedAt: occurredAt,
   332	        payload,
   333	      }
   334	      state.nextSequence += 1
   335	      this.emitEnvelope(taskId, envelope)
   336	    }
   337	    push('turn.created', { turnId })
   338	    push('message.accepted', { text: inputText, role: 'user' })
   339	    push('run.queued', {})
   340	    push('run.started', { source: 'voltagent-adapter' })
   341	  }
   342	
   343	  private async handleSubmitTurn(
   344	    command: SubmitTurnCommand,
   345	  ): Promise<CommandAcknowledgement> {
   346	    const taskId = command.taskId
   347	    const state = this.ensureTask(taskId)
   348	    if (state.activeAbort) {
   349	      return rejected(command.commandId, 'task_busy', '当前任务已有进行中的 Run')
   350	    }
   351	
   352	    const { turnId, runId } = this.allocateIds(taskId, command)
   353	    state.lastRunId = runId
   354	    state.lastTurnId = turnId
   355	    state.lastUserText = command.inputText
   356	    state.pendingApprovals.clear()
   357	    state.activeAbort = new AbortController()
   358	    const abort = state.activeAbort
   359	
   360	    this.pushBookkeeping(taskId, turnId, runId, command.inputText)
   361	
   362	    const ack = accepted(command.commandId, this.nowIso())
   363	
   364	    void this.streamAgent({
   365	      taskId,
   366	      turnId,
   367	      runId,
   368	      input: command.inputText,
   369	      signal: abort.signal,
   370	      completeIfNoTerminal: true,
   371	    }).finally(() => {
   372	      if (state.activeAbort === abort) state.activeAbort = null
   373	    })
   374	
   375	    return ack
   376	  }
   377	
   378	  private async handleCancel(
   379	    commandId: string,
   380	    taskId: string,

     5	function sseBody(chunks: object[]): ReadableStream<Uint8Array> {
     6	  const encoder = new TextEncoder()
     7	  const lines = chunks.map((c) => `data: ${JSON.stringify(c)}\n\n`).join('')
     8	  return new ReadableStream({
     9	    start(controller) {
    10	      controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(lines))
    11	      controller.close()
    12	    },
    13	  })
    14	}
    15	
    16	function collectEvents(
    17	  adapter: ReturnType<typeof createVoltAgentRuntimeAdapter>,
    18	  taskId: string,
    19	): RuntimeSubscriptionEvent[] {
    20	  const events: RuntimeSubscriptionEvent[] = []
    21	  adapter.subscribe(taskId, null, (e) => events.push(e))
    22	  return events
    23	}

title: Workspace Security
slug: /workspaces/security

# Workspace Security

:::warning Experimental
The Workspace API is experimental. Expect iteration and possible breaking changes as we refine the API.
:::

Workspace is powerful; treat it like a production capability. Recommended practices:

- **Filesystem containment**: Keep all file access within a workspace root. Avoid exposing absolute host paths.
- **Read-only mode**: Use `filesystem.readOnly` for agents that should never write.
- **Tool policies**: Require approvals for write/delete and sandbox execution in untrusted contexts.
- **Sandbox isolation**: Prefer `LocalSandbox` with isolation enabled (and a dedicated root directory).
- **Environment variables**: Avoid inheriting the full process environment; pass only what is needed.
- **Timeouts**: Set `operationTimeoutMs` (and sandbox `timeout_ms`) to prevent runaway tasks.
- **Direct search access**: Keep `search.allowDirectAccess` disabled unless you truly need it.
- **Skills allowlist**: Access to skill references/scripts/assets is allowlisted; avoid loading arbitrary files.

## Tool policy defaults

`toolConfig` lets you set workspace-level defaults; agent-level toolkit options merge on top:

```ts
const workspace = new Workspace({
  toolConfig: {
    filesystem: {
      defaults: { needsApproval: true },
      tools: { write_file: { enabled: false } },
    },
  },
});
```

`requireReadBeforeWrite` ensures the agent calls `read_file` on the path before it can modify or delete it. If the file changes after it was read, the tool will ask the agent to re-read it.

## Timeboxing operations

`operationTimeoutMs` applies to workspace tool executions (filesystem, sandbox, search, skills). You can override it per toolkit:

```ts
const agent = new Agent({
  name: "workspace-agent",
  model,
  workspace,
  workspaceToolkits: {
    filesystem: { operationTimeoutMs: 10_000 },
    search: { operationTimeoutMs: 5_000 },
  },
});
```

Combined example (timeboxed tools + isolated local sandbox):

```ts
const workspace = new Workspace({
  operationTimeoutMs: 30_000,
  sandbox: new LocalSandbox({
    rootDir: "/tmp/voltagent",
    isolation: {
      provider: "sandbox-exec",
      allowNetwork: false,
      readWritePaths: ["/tmp/voltagent"],
    },
  }),
});
```

---
title: Workspace Filesystem
slug: /workspaces/filesystem
---

# Workspace Filesystem

:::warning Experimental
The Workspace API is experimental. Expect iteration and possible breaking changes as we refine the API.
:::

Workspace filesystem provides a persistent (or in-memory) file layer for agents. Use it to store notes, datasets, and intermediate outputs.

## Backend options

In-memory is the default. For disk persistence, use `NodeFilesystemBackend`:

```ts
import { Workspace, NodeFilesystemBackend } from "@voltagent/core";

const workspace = new Workspace({
  filesystem: {
    backend: new NodeFilesystemBackend({
      rootDir: "./.workspace",
    }),
  },
});
```

All filesystem tool paths are workspace-relative and must start with `/`.

### Runtime context in backend factories

Custom backend factories receive `operationContext`, so you can route filesystem storage at runtime (for example, by tenant/account).

```ts
import { Workspace, NodeFilesystemBackend } from "@voltagent/core";

const workspace = new Workspace({
  filesystem: {
    backend: ({ operationContext }) => {
      const tenantId = String(operationContext?.context.get("tenantId") ?? "default");
      return new NodeFilesystemBackend({
        rootDir: `./.workspace/${tenantId}`,
      });
    },
  },
});
```

For production, sanitize tenant IDs before using them in file paths.

## Filesystem tools

- `ls`: list files in a directory
- `read_file`: read a file
- `write_file`: write a file
- `edit_file`: edit a file
- `delete_file`: delete a file
- `stat`: get file or directory metadata
- `mkdir`: create a directory
- `rmdir`: remove a directory
- `list_tree`: list files and directories recursively with depth control
- `list_files`: alias for `list_tree`
- `glob`: list files matching a glob
- `grep`: search for a regex pattern

## Behavior

- `write_file` supports `overwrite` and `create_parent_dirs`
- `delete_file` supports `recursive` to delete directories

## Read-only mode

Mark the filesystem as read-only to block writes and hide write tools from the toolkit:

```ts
const workspace = new Workspace({
  filesystem: {
    readOnly: true,
  },
});
```

When read-only, `workspace_index` and `workspace_index_content` are also disabled.

## Tool policies (filesystem)

Tool policies let you enable/disable tools, require approval, or enforce read-before-write:

```ts
const agent = new Agent({
  name: "workspace-agent",
  model,
  workspace,
  workspaceToolkits: {
    filesystem: {
      toolPolicies: {
        defaults: { needsApproval: true },
        tools: {
          write_file: { enabled: false },
          edit_file: { needsApproval: true },
          delete_file: { requireReadBeforeWrite: true },
          mkdir: { requireReadBeforeWrite: true },
        },
      },
    },
  },
});
```

`requireReadBeforeWrite` forces a `read_file` call before edits/deletes and prompts a re-read if the file changes.

## Security notes

- Keep filesystem access within a workspace root.
- Avoid exposing absolute host paths.
- Prefer read-only mode for untrusted agents.

For broader recommendations, see [Workspace Security](../security).

// src/workspace/filesystem/backends/filesystem.ts
var SUPPORTS_NOFOLLOW = fsSync.constants.O_NOFOLLOW !== void 0;
var fastGlobPromise;
async function loadFastGlob() {
  if (!fastGlobPromise) {
    fastGlobPromise = import("fast-glob").then((mod) => {
      const resolved = mod.default ?? mod;
      return resolved;
    });
  }
  return fastGlobPromise;
}
__name(loadFastGlob, "loadFastGlob");
var NodeFilesystemBackend = class {
  static {
    __name(this, "NodeFilesystemBackend");
  }
  cwd;
  virtualMode;
  contained;
  maxFileSizeBytes;
  constructor(options = {}) {
    const { rootDir, virtualMode = true, contained = true, maxFileSizeMb = 10 } = options;
    this.cwd = rootDir ? path2.resolve(rootDir) : process.cwd();
    this.virtualMode = virtualMode;
    this.contained = contained;
    this.maxFileSizeBytes = maxFileSizeMb * 1024 * 1024;
  }
  resolvePath(key) {
    const trimmed = key.trim();
    if (!trimmed) {
      throw new Error("Path cannot be empty");
    }
    const normalized = trimmed.replace(/\\/g, "/");
    const cleaned = normalized.replace(/^\/+/, "");
    const normalizedInput = path2.normalize(cleaned);
    if (this.virtualMode || this.contained) {
      const full = path2.resolve(this.cwd, normalizedInput);
      if (this.contained) {
        const relative3 = path2.relative(this.cwd, full);
        if (relative3.startsWith("..") || path2.isAbsolute(relative3)) {
          throw new Error(`Path: ${full} outside root directory: ${this.cwd}`);
        }
      }
      return full;
    }
    if (path2.isAbsolute(normalized)) {
      return path2.resolve(normalized);
    }
    return path2.resolve(this.cwd, normalized);
  }
  isEnoentError(error) {
    if (!error || typeof error !== "object") {
      return false;
    }
    return "code" in error && error.code === "ENOENT";
  }
  async assertPathContained(absolutePath) {
    if (!this.contained) {
      return;
    }
    let baseReal;
    try {
      baseReal = await fs2.realpath(this.cwd);
    } catch (error) {
      if (this.isEnoentError(error)) {
        throw new Error(`Root directory not found: ${this.cwd}`);
      }
      throw error;
    }
    let targetReal;
    try {
      targetReal = await fs2.realpath(absolutePath);
    } catch (error) {
      if (this.isEnoentError(error)) {
        let parentPath = absolutePath;
        while (true) {
          const nextParent = path2.dirname(parentPath);
          if (nextParent === parentPath) {
            throw new Error(`Path: ${absolutePath} outside root directory: ${this.cwd}`);
          }
          parentPath = nextParent;
          try {
            targetReal = await fs2.realpath(parentPath);
            break;
          } catch (parentError) {
            if (!this.isEnoentError(parentError)) {
              throw parentError;
            }
          }
        }
      } else {
        throw error;
      }
    }
    if (!targetReal) {
      throw new Error(`Path: ${absolutePath} outside root directory: ${this.cwd}`);
    }
    if (targetReal !== baseReal && !targetReal.startsWith(`${baseReal}${path2.sep}`)) {
      throw new Error(`Path: ${absolutePath} outside root directory: ${this.cwd}`);
    }
  }
  toVirtualPath(fullPath, isDir) {
    const cwdStr = this.cwd.endsWith(path2.sep) ? this.cwd : this.cwd + path2.sep;
    let relativePath;
    if (fullPath.startsWith(cwdStr)) {
      relativePath = fullPath.substring(cwdStr.length);
    } else if (fullPath.startsWith(this.cwd)) {
      relativePath = fullPath.substring(this.cwd.length).replace(/^[/\\]/, "");
      const stat = await fs2.lstat(resolvedPath);
      if (stat.isSymbolicLink()) {
        throw new Error(`Symlinks are not allowed: ${filePath}`);
      }
      const isDir = stat.isDirectory();
      const pathValue = this.virtualMode ? this.toVirtualPath(resolvedPath, isDir) : isDir ? resolvedPath.endsWith(path2.sep) ? resolvedPath : `${resolvedPath}${path2.sep}` : resolvedPath;
      return {
        path: pathValue,
        is_dir: isDir,
        size: isDir ? 0 : stat.size,
        modified_at: stat.mtime.toISOString(),
        created_at: stat.ctime.toISOString()
      };
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }
  async exists(filePath) {
    try {
      const resolvedPath = this.resolvePath(filePath);
      await this.assertPathContained(resolvedPath);
      const stat = await fs2.lstat(resolvedPath);
      if (stat.isSymbolicLink()) {
        return false;
      }
      return stat.isFile() || stat.isDirectory();
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }
  async write(filePath, content, options) {
    try {
      const resolvedPath = this.resolvePath(filePath);
      await this.assertPathContained(resolvedPath);
      const overwrite = options?.overwrite ?? false;
      try {
        const stat = await fs2.lstat(resolvedPath);
        if (stat.isSymbolicLink()) {
          return {
            error: `Cannot write to ${filePath} because it is a symlink. Symlinks are not allowed.`
          };
        }
        if (!stat.isFile()) {
          return {
            error: `Cannot write to ${filePath} because it is not a file.`
          };
        }
        if (!overwrite) {
          return {
            error: `Cannot write to ${filePath} because it already exists. Read and then make an edit, or write to a new path.`
          };
        }
      } catch {
      }
      await fs2.mkdir(path2.dirname(resolvedPath), { recursive: true });
      if (SUPPORTS_NOFOLLOW) {
        const flags = fsSync.constants.O_WRONLY | fsSync.constants.O_CREAT | fsSync.constants.O_TRUNC | fsSync.constants.O_NOFOLLOW;
        const fd = await fs2.open(resolvedPath, flags, 420);
        try {
          await fd.writeFile(content, "utf-8");
        } finally {
          await fd.close();
        }
      } else {
        try {
          const stat = await fs2.lstat(resolvedPath);
          if (stat.isSymbolicLink()) {
            return {
              error: `Cannot write to ${filePath} because it is a symlink. Symlinks are not allowed.`
            };
          }
          if (!stat.isFile()) {
            return {
              error: `Cannot write to ${filePath} because it is not a file.`
            };
          }
          if (!overwrite) {
            return {
              error: `Cannot write to ${filePath} because it already exists. Read and then make an edit, or write to a new path.`
            };
          }
        } catch {
        }
        await fs2.writeFile(resolvedPath, content, "utf-8");
      }
      return { path: filePath, filesUpdate: null };
    mkdir(path: string, recursive?: boolean): Promise<MkdirResult>;
    rmdir(path: string, recursive?: boolean): Promise<RmdirResult>;
}

declare class NodeFilesystemBackend implements FilesystemBackend {
    private cwd;
    private virtualMode;
    private readonly contained;
    private maxFileSizeBytes;
    constructor(options?: {
        rootDir?: string;
        virtualMode?: boolean;
        contained?: boolean;
        maxFileSizeMb?: number;
    });
    private resolvePath;
    private isEnoentError;
    private assertPathContained;
    private toVirtualPath;
    lsInfo(dirPath: string): Promise<FileInfo[]>;
    read(filePath: string, offset?: number, limit?: number): Promise<string>;
    readRaw(filePath: string): Promise<FileData>;
    stat(filePath: string): Promise<FileInfo | null>;
    exists(filePath: string): Promise<boolean>;
    write(filePath: string, content: string, options?: WriteOptions): Promise<WriteResult>;
    mkdir(dirPath: string, recursive?: boolean): Promise<MkdirResult>;
    edit(filePath: string, oldString: string, newString: string, replaceAll?: boolean): Promise<EditResult>;
    delete(filePath: string, options?: DeleteOptions): Promise<DeleteResult>;
    rmdir(dirPath: string, recursive?: boolean): Promise<RmdirResult>;
    grepRaw(pattern: string, dirPath?: string, glob?: string | null): Promise<GrepMatch[] | string>;
    private ripgrepSearch;
    private fallbackSearch;
    globInfo(pattern: string, searchPath?: string): Promise<FileInfo[]>;
}

declare class InMemoryFilesystemBackend implements FilesystemBackend {

    17	function resolveSafePath(relativePath: string): string {
    18	  const root = workspaceRoot()
    19	  const resolved = path.resolve(root, relativePath)
    20	  const rel = path.relative(root, resolved)
    21	  if (rel.startsWith('..') || path.isAbsolute(rel)) {
    22	    throw new Error(`路径越界：仅允许工作区根内文件（${root}）`)
    23	  }
    24	  return resolved
    25	}
    26	
    27	export const readFileTool = createTool({
    28	  name: 'read_file',
    29	  description:
    30	    'Read a UTF-8 text file under the configured workspace root. Path is relative to workspace root.',
    31	  parameters: z.object({
    32	    path: z.string().describe('Relative path under workspace root'),
    33	  }),
    34	  execute: async ({ path: rel }) => {
    35	    const abs = resolveSafePath(rel)
    36	    const content = await readFile(abs, 'utf8')
    37	    return {
    38	      path: rel,
    39	      content: content.length > 20_000 ? `${content.slice(0, 20_000)}\n…(truncated)` : content,
    40	      bytes: content.length,
    41	    }
    42	  },
    43	})
    44	
    45	export const writeFileTool = createTool({
    46	  name: 'write_file',
    47	  description:
    48	    'Write a UTF-8 text file under the workspace root. Creates parent directories. Path is relative.',
    49	  parameters: z.object({
    50	    path: z.string().describe('Relative path under workspace root'),
    51	    content: z.string().describe('Full file content'),
    52	  }),
    53	  needsApproval: true,
    54	  execute: async ({ path: rel, content }) => {
    55	    const abs = resolveSafePath(rel)
    56	    await mkdir(path.dirname(abs), { recursive: true })
    57	    await writeFile(abs, content, 'utf8')
    58	    const lines = content.split('\n').length
    59	    return {
    60	      path: rel,
    61	      additions: lines,
    62	      deletions: 0,
    63	      bytes: Buffer.byteLength(content, 'utf8'),
    64	    }
    65	  },
    66	})
    67	
    68	export const runCommandTool = createTool({
    69	  name: 'run_command',
    70	  description:
    71	    'Echo-only demo command tool (does not spawn a real shell). Use for Timeline command-row demos.',
    72	  parameters: z.object({
    73	    command: z.string().describe('Command string to acknowledge'),
    74	  }),
    75	  needsApproval: async ({ command }) =>
    76	    /\brm\b|\bsudo\b|\bmkfs\b|\bdd\b/i.test(command),
    77	  execute: async ({ command }) => {
    78	    return {
    79	      command,
    80	      stdout: `[workbench-runtime-voltagent] acknowledged: ${command}`,
    81	      exitCode: 0,
    82	      note: 'Demo tool — no real process was spawned.',
    83	    }
    84	  },
    85	})
    86	
    87	export const workbenchTools = [readFileTool, writeFileTool, runCommandTool]
     1	/**
     2	 * Build the Workbench sidecar Agent for the selected profile.
     3	 * Office → Agent + Workspace (Node FS, write/delete need approval).
     4	 * Minimal → plain Agent + DIY tools (legacy M3).
     5	 */
     6	
     7	import { mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises'
     8	import {
     9	  Agent,
    10	  NodeFilesystemBackend,
    11	  Workspace,
    12	  type Tool,
    13	  type Toolkit,
    14	} from '@voltagent/core'
    15	import type { LanguageModel } from 'ai'
    16	import {
    17	  type AgentProfile,
    18	  resolveWorkspaceRoot,
    19	  toolsForProfile,
    20	} from './profile.js'
    21	import { workbenchTools } from './tools.js'
    22	
    23	export type CreateWorkbenchAgentOptions = {
    24	  profile: AgentProfile
    25	  model: LanguageModel
    26	  env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv
    27	  /** Override workspace root (tests). */
    28	  workspaceRoot?: string
    29	  maxSteps?: number
    30	}
    31	
    32	export type WorkbenchAgentBundle = {
    33	  profile: AgentProfile
    34	  agent: Agent
    35	  workspaceRoot: string
    36	  tools: readonly string[]
    37	  /** Present only for office profile. */
    38	  workspace?: Workspace
    39	}
    40	
    41	/**
    42	 * Filesystem tool policies: reads free; write/edit/delete/rmdir need approval.
    43	 */
    44	export function officeFilesystemToolConfig() {
    45	  return {
    46	    filesystem: {
    47	      defaults: { needsApproval: false },
    48	      tools: {
    49	        write_file: { needsApproval: true },
    50	        edit_file: { needsApproval: true, requireReadBeforeWrite: true },
    51	        delete_file: { needsApproval: true, requireReadBeforeWrite: true },
    52	        rmdir: { needsApproval: true },
    53	      },
    54	    },
    55	  }
    56	}
    57	
    58	export async function createWorkbenchAgent(
    59	  options: CreateWorkbenchAgentOptions,
    60	): Promise<WorkbenchAgentBundle> {
    61	  const env = options.env ?? process.env
    62	  const profile = options.profile
    63	  const workspaceRoot =
    64	    options.workspaceRoot ?? resolveWorkspaceRoot(env, profile)
    65	  const tools = toolsForProfile(profile)
    66	
    67	  if (profile === 'office') {
    68	    await mkdir(workspaceRoot, { recursive: true })
    69	
    70	    const workspace = new Workspace({
    71	      id: 'workbench-office',
    72	      name: 'Workbench Office Workspace',
    73	      operationTimeoutMs: Number(env.WORKSPACE_OPERATION_TIMEOUT_MS ?? 30_000),
    74	      filesystem: {
    75	        backend: new NodeFilesystemBackend({
    76	          rootDir: workspaceRoot,
    77	          virtualMode: true,
    78	          contained: true,
    79	        }),
    80	      },
    81	      toolConfig: officeFilesystemToolConfig(),
    82	    })
    83	
    84	    const agent = new Agent({
    85	      id: 'workbench',
    86	      name: 'workbench',
    87	      purpose: '本机办公 Agent Runtime（Workspace FS）',
    88	      instructions: [
    89	        'You are the local Office Agent Runtime for UI Lab Agent Workbench.',
    90	        'Respond in Chinese unless the user writes in another language.',
    91	        'Use Workspace filesystem tools (ls, read_file, write_file, edit_file, …) inside the authorized root.',
    92	        'All file paths must be virtual workspace paths starting with / (e.g. /notes/a.md, /output/report.md).',
    93	        'Never use host absolute paths (/Users/..., /home/..., drive letters). Never paste operator host paths into tools.',
    94	        'Prefer planning briefly, then read before write. Writes and deletes require user approval.',
    95	        'Do not claim to be a remote multi-tenant production cluster — this is a local office sidecar.',
    96	      ].join(' '),
    97	      model: options.model,
    98	      workspace,
    99	      // O1: FS only — no sandbox / search / skills (O3–O5 / later tickets).
   100	      workspaceToolkits: {
   101	        filesystem: {},
   102	        sandbox: false,
   103	        search: false,
   104	        skills: false,
   105	      },
   106	      maxSteps: options.maxSteps ?? Number(env.VOLTAGENT_MAX_STEPS ?? 50),
   107	    })
   108	
   109	    return { profile, agent, workspaceRoot, tools, workspace }
   110	  }
   111	
   112	  // minimal — DIY tools (M1–M3 baseline)
   113	  const agent = new Agent({
   114	    id: 'workbench',
   115	    name: 'workbench',
   116	    purpose: '本机最小 Agent Runtime（DIY 工具）',
   117	    instructions: [
   118	      'You are the local Agent Runtime for UI Lab Agent Workbench.',
   119	      'Respond in Chinese unless the user writes in another language.',
   120	      'You may use read_file, write_file (requires approval), and run_command tools when helpful.',
   121	      'Prefer concise answers. Stay within the workspace tools for file access.',
   122	      'This is a local demo sidecar, not a remote production cluster.',
   123	    ].join(' '),
   124	    model: options.model,
   125	    tools: workbenchTools as (Tool<any, any> | Toolkit)[],
   126	    maxSteps: options.maxSteps ?? Number(env.VOLTAGENT_MAX_STEPS ?? 12),
   127	  })
   128	
   129	  return { profile, agent, workspaceRoot, tools }
   130	}

__name(isFeedbackProvided, "isFeedbackProvided");
function isMessageFeedbackProvided(message) {
  if (!message || !isRecord3(message.metadata)) {
    return false;
  }
  const rawFeedback = message.metadata.feedback;
  if (!isRecord3(rawFeedback)) {
    return false;
  }
  return isFeedbackProvided(rawFeedback);
}
__name(isMessageFeedbackProvided, "isMessageFeedbackProvided");
function resolveFeedbackProvidedAt(value) {
  if (value instanceof Date) {
    if (Number.isNaN(value.getTime())) {
      throw new Error("markFeedbackProvided received an invalid Date value");
    }
    return value.toISOString();
  }
  if (typeof value === "string") {
    const trimmed = value.trim();
    if (!trimmed) {
      return (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
    }
    const parsed = new Date(trimmed);
    if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {
      throw new Error("markFeedbackProvided received an invalid providedAt timestamp");
    }
    return parsed.toISOString();
  }
  return (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
}
__name(resolveFeedbackProvidedAt, "resolveFeedbackProvidedAt");
async function markFeedbackProvided(params) {
  const { memory, input } = params;
  const userId = typeof input.userId === "string" ? input.userId.trim() : "";
  const conversationId = typeof input.conversationId === "string" ? input.conversationId.trim() : "";
  const messageId = typeof input.messageId === "string" ? input.messageId.trim() : "";
  if (!userId) {
    throw new Error("markFeedbackProvided requires a non-empty userId");
  }
  if (!conversationId) {
    throw new Error("markFeedbackProvided requires a non-empty conversationId");
  }
  if (!messageId) {
    throw new Error("markFeedbackProvided requires a non-empty messageId");
  }
  if (!memory) {
    throw new Error("Cannot mark feedback as provided because memory is not configured");
  }
  const messages = await memory.getMessages(userId, conversationId);
  const target = messages.find((message) => message.id === messageId);
  if (!target) {
    return null;
  }
  const messageMetadata = typeof target.metadata === "object" && target.metadata !== null ? target.metadata : {};
  const rawFeedback = messageMetadata.feedback;

    ...existingFeedback,
    provided: true,
    providedAt,
    ...providedFeedbackId ? { feedbackId: providedFeedbackId } : {}

function findFeedbackMessageId(messages, feedback) {
  if (messages.length === 0) {
    return void 0;
  }
  const feedbackTokenId = typeof feedback.tokenId === "string" ? feedback.tokenId.trim() : void 0;
  const feedbackTraceId = typeof feedback.traceId === "string" ? feedback.traceId.trim() : "";
  const feedbackKey = typeof feedback.key === "string" ? feedback.key.trim() : "";
  const feedbackUrl = typeof feedback.url === "string" ? feedback.url.trim() : "";
  for (let index = messages.length - 1; index >= 0; index--) {
    const message = messages[index];
    if (message.role !== "assistant") {
      continue;
    }
    if (!hasNonEmptyString(message.id)) {
      continue;
    }
    if (!isRecord3(message.metadata)) {
      continue;
    }
    const rawFeedback = message.metadata.feedback;
    if (!isRecord3(rawFeedback)) {
      continue;
    }
    const messageTokenId = typeof rawFeedback.tokenId === "string" ? rawFeedback.tokenId.trim() : void 0;
    if (feedbackTokenId && messageTokenId === feedbackTokenId) {
      return message.id;
    }
    const messageTraceId = typeof rawFeedback.traceId === "string" ? rawFeedback.traceId.trim() : "";
    const messageKey = typeof rawFeedback.key === "string" ? rawFeedback.key.trim() : "";
    const messageUrl = typeof rawFeedback.url === "string" ? rawFeedback.url.trim() : "";
    if (messageTraceId === feedbackTraceId && messageKey === feedbackKey && messageUrl === feedbackUrl) {
      return message.id;
    }
  }
  return void 0;
}

var AGENT_METADATA_CONTEXT_KEY = Symbol("agentMetadata");
var SUBAGENT_TOOL_CALL_METADATA_KEY = Symbol("subAgentToolCallMetadata");
var MemoryPersistQueue = class {
  constructor(memoryManager, options = {}) {
    this.memoryManager = memoryManager;
    this.debounceMs = options.debounceMs ?? 200;
    this.logger = options.logger;
  }
  static {
    __name(this, "MemoryPersistQueue");
  }
  debounceMs;
  logger;
  entries = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  scheduleSave(buffer, oc) {
    if (!oc.conversationId || !oc.userId) {
      return;
    }
    const key = this.getKey(oc);
    const entry = this.getOrCreateEntry(key);
    if (entry.timer) {
      clearTimeout(entry.timer);
    }
    entry.timer = setTimeout(() => {
      entry.timer = void 0;
      this.enqueuePersist(key, () => this.persist(buffer, oc));
    }, this.debounceMs);
    const logPayload = {
      conversationId: oc.conversationId,
      userId: oc.userId
    };
    this.logger?.debug?.("[MemoryPersistQueue] schedule", logPayload);
  }
  async flush(buffer, oc) {
    if (!oc.conversationId || !oc.userId) return;
    const key = this.getKey(oc);
    const entry = this.getOrCreateEntry(key);
    if (entry.timer) {
      clearTimeout(entry.timer);
      entry.timer = void 0;
    }
    const flushPayload = {
      conversationId: oc.conversationId,
      userId: oc.userId
    };
    this.logger?.debug?.("Flushing conversation persistence queue", flushPayload);
    await this.enqueuePersist(key, () => this.persist(buffer, oc));
  }
  async persist(buffer, oc) {
    if (!oc.userId || !oc.conversationId) {
      return;
    }
    const pending = buffer.drainPendingMessages();
    if (pending.length === 0) {
      const payload2 = {
        conversationId: oc.conversationId,
        userId: oc.userId
      };
      this.logger?.debug?.("[MemoryPersistQueue] nothing-to-persist", payload2);
      return;
    }
    const payload = {
      conversationId: oc.conversationId,
      userId: oc.userId,
      count: pending.length,
      ids: pending.map((msg) => msg.id)
    };
    this.logger?.debug?.("[MemoryPersistQueue] persisting", payload);
    const agentMetadata = oc.systemContext.get(AGENT_METADATA_CONTEXT_KEY);
    const shouldApplySubAgentMetadata = Boolean(agentMetadata && oc.parentAgentId);
    const toolCallMetadata = oc.systemContext.get(SUBAGENT_TOOL_CALL_METADATA_KEY);
    for (const message of pending) {
      try {
        const messageWithMetadata = this.applySubAgentMetadata(message, {
          defaultMetadata: shouldApplySubAgentMetadata ? agentMetadata : void 0,
          toolCallMetadata
        });
        await this.memoryManager.saveMessage(oc, messageWithMetadata, oc.userId, oc.conversationId);
      } catch (error) {
        this.logger?.error?.("Failed to save message", {
          conversationId: oc.conversationId,
          userId: oc.userId,
          error
        });
        throw error;
      }
    }
  }
  enqueuePersist(key, task) {
    const entry = this.getOrCreateEntry(key);
    entry.pendingPromise = entry.pendingPromise.catch(() => {
    }).then(async () => {
      await task();
    }).catch((error) => {
      this.logger?.error?.("Failed to persist conversation messages", { error });
      throw error;
    }).finally(() => {
      const current = this.entries.get(key);
      if (current === entry && !current?.timer) {
        this.entries.delete(key);
      }
    });
    return entry.pendingPromise;
  }
  getOrCreateEntry(key) {
    let entry = this.entries.get(key);
    if (!entry) {
      entry = { pendingPromise: Promise.resolve() };
      this.entries.set(key, entry);
    }
    return entry;
  }
  getKey(oc) {
    return `${oc.userId ?? "unknown"}:${oc.conversationId ?? "unknown"}`;
  }
  applySubAgentMetadata(message, opts) {
    let metadata = typeof message.metadata === "object" && message.metadata !== null ? { ...message.metadata } : void 0;
    const attachMetadata = /* @__PURE__ */ __name((value) => {
      if (!value) return;
      if (!metadata) metadata = {};
      if (!metadata.subAgentId) {
        metadata.subAgentId = value.agentId;
      }
      if (!metadata.subAgentName) {
        metadata.subAgentName = value.agentName;
      }
    }, "attachMetadata");
    const partMetadata = opts.toolCallMetadata && this.getMetadataFromMessageParts(message, opts.toolCallMetadata);
    if (partMetadata) {
      attachMetadata(partMetadata);
    }
    if (!metadata?.subAgentId && opts.defaultMetadata) {
      attachMetadata(opts.defaultMetadata);
    }
    if (!metadata) {
      return message;
    }
    return {
      ...message,
      metadata
    };
  }
  getMetadataFromMessageParts(message, toolCallMetadata) {
    for (const part of message.parts) {
      const type = part.type;
      if (!type) continue;
      if (type === "data-subagent-stream") {
        const data = part.data;
        if (data?.subAgentId && data?.subAgentName) {
          return { agentId: data.subAgentId, agentName: data.subAgentName };
        }
        continue;
      }
      if (type.startsWith("tool-")) {
        const toolCallId = part.toolCallId;
        if (toolCallId && toolCallMetadata.has(toolCallId)) {
          return toolCallMetadata.get(toolCallId);
        }
      }
    }
    return void 0;
  }
};

    45	    assert.equal(cfg.filesystem.tools.delete_file.needsApproval, true)
    46	    assert.equal(cfg.filesystem.tools.rmdir.needsApproval, true)
    47	  })
    48	})
    49	
    50	describe('createWorkbenchAgent', () => {
    51	  it('office profile mounts Workspace FS and does not use DIY run_command', async () => {
    52	    const root = await mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'wb-office-'))
    53	    tempRoots.push(root)
    54	
    55	    const bundle = await createWorkbenchAgent({
    56	      profile: 'office',
    57	      model: stubModel,
    58	      workspaceRoot: root,
    59	      maxSteps: 20,
    60	      env: {
    61	        VOLTAGENT_MEMORY: 'in-memory',
    62	      } as NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
    63	    })
    64	
    65	    assert.equal(bundle.profile, 'office')
    66	    assert.equal(bundle.workspaceRoot, root)
    67	    assert.equal(bundle.maxSteps, 20)
    68	    assert.equal(bundle.summarizationEnabled, true)
    69	    assert.equal(bundle.memoryKind, 'in-memory')
    70	    assert.ok(bundle.workspace, 'workspace instance present')
    71	    assert.ok(bundle.tools.includes('ls'))
    72	    assert.ok(bundle.tools.includes('write_file'))
    73	    assert.ok(!bundle.tools.includes('run_command'))
    74	
    75	    // O2 first-run bootstrap
    76	    const readmePath = path.join(root, OFFICE_WORKSPACE_README_NAME)
    77	    await access(readmePath)
    78	    const readme = await readFile(readmePath, 'utf8')
    79	    assert.match(readme, /WORKSPACE_ROOT/)
    80	
    81	    // O3 skills seed
    82	    const skillIds = await listSeededSkillIds(root)
    83	    assert.deepEqual(skillIds, [...OFFICE_SKILL_IDS].sort())
    84	    for (const rel of OFFICE_OUTPUT_DIRS) {
    85	      await access(path.join(root, rel))
    86	    }
    87	
    88	    // Agent carries workspace identity; tool names come from Workspace toolkit.
    89	    assert.equal(bundle.agent.id, 'workbench')
    90	    const fullState = await bundle.agent.getFullState()
    91	    const toolNames = (fullState.tools ?? []).map(
    92	      (t: { name?: string }) => t.name,
    93	    )
    94	    assert.ok(
    95	      toolNames.includes('read_file') || toolNames.includes('ls'),
    96	      `expected Workspace FS tools, got: ${toolNames.join(',')}`,
    97	    )
    98	    assert.ok(
    99	      toolNames.includes('workspace_list_skills') ||
   100	        toolNames.includes('workspace_activate_skill'),
   101	      `expected skills toolkit tools, got: ${toolNames.join(',')}`,
   102	    )
   103	    assert.ok(
   104	      !toolNames.includes('run_command'),
   105	      'DIY run_command must not be primary tools in office profile',
   106	    )
   107	
   108	    // Discover skills via Workspace API (no LLM).
   109	    assert.ok(bundle.workspace?.skills, 'workspace.skills present')
   110	    const discovered = await bundle.workspace!.skills!.discoverSkills({
   111	      refresh: true,
   112	    })
   113	    const discoveredNames = discovered.map((s) => s.name).sort()
   114	    for (const id of OFFICE_SKILL_IDS) {
   115	      assert.ok(
   116	        discoveredNames.includes(id) ||
   117	          discovered.some((s) => s.id.includes(id) || s.path.includes(id)),
   118	        `expected skill ${id} in ${JSON.stringify(discovered)}`,
   119	      )
   120	    }
   121	
   122	    // Activate + read → write deliverable path (simulates skill E2E without LLM).
   123	    const meta = await bundle.workspace!.skills!.activateSkill('meeting-notes')
   124	    assert.ok(meta, 'activate meeting-notes')
   125	    const loaded = await bundle.workspace!.skills!.loadSkill('meeting-notes')
   126	    assert.ok(loaded?.instructions.includes('output/meeting-notes'))
   127	
   128	    const deliverable = path.join(
   129	      root,
   130	      'output/meeting-notes',
   131	      'test-notes.md',
   132	    )
   133	    await writeFile(
   134	      deliverable,
   135	      '# 测试纪要\n\n- 决议：O3 skills 可用\n',
   136	      'utf8',
   137	    )
   138	    await access(deliverable)
   139	    await bundle.disconnectMcp()
   140	  })
   141	
   142	  it('office O5 defaults: maxSteps ≥ 50, summarization on, memory available', async () => {
   143	    const root = await mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'wb-office-o5-'))
   144	    tempRoots.push(root)
   145	
   146	    const bundle = await createWorkbenchAgent({
   147	      profile: 'office',
   148	      model: stubModel,
   149	      workspaceRoot: root,
   150	      env: {
   151	        VOLTAGENT_MEMORY: 'in-memory',
   152	      } as NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
   153	    })
   154	
   155	    assert.ok(bundle.maxSteps >= 50)
   156	    assert.ok(bundle.maxSteps >= 80)
   157	    assert.equal(bundle.summarizationEnabled, true)
   158	    assert.equal(bundle.memoryKind, 'in-memory')
   159	    // O4: no MCP env → both disabled; FS tools still present
   160	    assert.match(bundle.mcpStatusLine, /docs=off/)
   161	    assert.match(bundle.mcpStatusLine, /calendar=off/)
   162	    assert.ok(bundle.tools.includes('ls'))
   163	    await bundle.disconnectMcp()
   164	  })
   165	
   166	  it('minimal profile keeps DIY tools without Workspace', async () => {
   167	    const bundle = await createWorkbenchAgent({
   168	      profile: 'minimal',
   169	      model: stubModel,
   170	      workspaceRoot: '/tmp/unused-minimal-root',
   171	      maxSteps: 8,
   172	    })
   173	
   174	    assert.equal(bundle.profile, 'minimal')
   175	    assert.equal(bundle.workspace, undefined)
   176	    assert.equal(bundle.maxSteps, 8)
   177	    assert.equal(bundle.summarizationEnabled, false)
   178	    assert.deepEqual([...bundle.tools], [
   179	      'read_file',
   180	      'write_file',
   181	      'run_command',
   182	    ])
   183	
   184	    const fullState = await bundle.agent.getFullState()
   185	    const toolNames = (fullState.tools ?? []).map(

    47	    assert.equal((b!.server as { url: string }).url, 'https://mcp.example/feishu-docs')
    48	  })
    49	
    50	  it('resolves calendar stdio command', () => {
    51	    const c = resolveMcpConnector('calendar', {
    52	      MCP_CALENDAR_COMMAND: 'npx',
    53	      MCP_CALENDAR_ARGS: '-y,@example/calendar-mcp',
    54	    })
    55	    assert.ok(c)
    56	    assert.equal(c!.transport, 'stdio')
    57	    assert.equal((c!.server as { type: string }).type, 'stdio')
    58	    assert.equal((c!.server as { command: string }).command, 'npx')
    59	  })
    60	})
    61	
    62	describe('resolveAllMcpConnectors', () => {
    63	  it('can enable both without conflict', () => {
    64	    const all = resolveAllMcpConnectors({
    65	      MCP_DOCS_URL: 'https://mcp.example/docs',
    66	      MCP_CALENDAR_URL: 'https://mcp.example/cal',
    67	    })
    68	    assert.equal(all.length, 2)
    69	    assert.deepEqual(
    70	      all.map((x) => x.id).sort(),
    71	      ['calendar', 'docs'],
    72	    )
    73	  })
    74	})
    75	
    76	describe('loadOfficeMcpTools', () => {
    77	  it('returns disabled statuses and empty tools without config', async () => {
    78	    const result = await loadOfficeMcpTools({})
    79	    assert.equal(result.tools.length, 0)
    80	    assert.equal(result.toolNames.length, 0)
    81	    assert.equal(result.statuses.length, 2)
    82	    assert.ok(result.statuses.every((s) => s.status === 'disabled'))
    83	    await result.disconnect()
    84	  })
    85	
    86	  it('connects via host mock and marks write tools for approval', async () => {
    87	    const result = await loadOfficeMcpTools(
    88	      {
    89	        MCP_DOCS_URL: 'https://mcp.example/docs',
    90	        MCP_CALENDAR_URL: 'https://mcp.example/cal',
    91	      },
    92	      {
    93	        host: {
    94	          getTools: async (servers) => {
    95	            const ids = Object.keys(servers)
    96	            const tools = ids.flatMap((id) => [
    97	              {
    98	                name: `${id}_read_item`,
    99	                description: 'read',
   100	              },
   101	              {
   102	                name: `${id}_write_item`,
   103	                description: 'write',
   104	              },
   105	            ]) as any[]
   106	            return {
   107	              tools,
   108	              disconnect: async () => {},
   109	            }
   110	          },
   111	        },
   112	      },
   113	    )
   114	
   115	    assert.equal(result.statuses.filter((s) => s.status === 'connected').length, 2)
   116	    assert.ok(result.toolNames.includes('docs_read_item'))
   117	    assert.ok(result.toolNames.includes('calendar_write_item'))
   118	    const write = result.tools.find((t) => t.name === 'docs_write_item') as {
   119	      needsApproval?: boolean
   120	    }
   121	    const read = result.tools.find((t) => t.name === 'docs_read_item') as {
   122	      needsApproval?: boolean
   123	    }
   124	    assert.equal(write?.needsApproval, true)
   125	    assert.notEqual(read?.needsApproval, true)
   126	    await result.disconnect()
   127	  })
   128	
   129	  it('degrades on host failure without throwing', async () => {
   130	    const result = await loadOfficeMcpTools(
   131	      { MCP_DOCS_URL: 'https://mcp.example/docs' },
   132	      {
   133	        host: {
   134	          getTools: async () => {
   135	            throw new Error('ECONNREFUSED mock')
   136	          },
   137	        },
   138	      },
   139	    )
   140	    assert.equal(result.tools.length, 0)
   141	    const docs = result.statuses.find((s) => s.id === 'docs')
   142	    assert.equal(docs?.status, 'failed')
   143	    assert.match(docs?.reason ?? '', /ECONNREFUSED|连接失败/)
   144	    // calendar still reported disabled
   145	    assert.equal(
   146	      result.statuses.find((s) => s.id === 'calendar')?.status,
   147	      'disabled',
   148	    )
   149	    await result.disconnect()
   150	  })
   151	})
   152	
   153	describe('applyMcpNeedsApproval + formatMcpStatusLine', () => {
   154	  it('formats status line for logs', () => {
   155	    const line = formatMcpStatusLine([
   156	      { id: 'docs', status: 'connected', toolNames: ['a', 'b'] },
   157	      { id: 'calendar', status: 'failed', toolNames: [], reason: 'x' },
   158	    ])
   159	    assert.equal(line, 'docs=ok(2),calendar=fail')
   160	  })
   161	
   162	  it('applyMcpNeedsApproval is idempotent on free tools', () => {
   163	    const tools = [{ name: 'list_events' }] as any[]
   164	    applyMcpNeedsApproval(tools)
   165	    assert.notEqual(tools[0].needsApproval, true)
   166	  })
   167	})

     1	# Operator Guide — Workbench VoltAgent Office Runtime
     2	
     3	本机 **Office Profile** 侧车操作说明。  
     4	**诚实边界：** 这是本地开发用 Agent Runtime（Workspace FS + Skills + 可选 MCP），**不是**多租户远程生产集群。Fake Runtime 与 capture 回放仍是一等公民。
     5	
     6	Workspace API 在 VoltAgent 中为 **Experimental**，升级时可能变化。
     7	
     8	---
     9	
    10	## 1. 从零演示（读写 + 审批 + Skill 交付物）
    11	
    12	### 1.1 依赖
    13	
    14	- Node 20+
    15	- monorepo `pnpm install`
    16	- DeepSeek（或兼容）API Key
    17	
    18	### 1.2 配置侧车
    19	
    20	```bash
    21	cd tooling/workbench-runtime-voltagent
    22	cp .env.example .env
    23	```
    24	
    25	最小 office `.env` 示例：
    26	
    27	```bash
    28	DEEPSEEK_API_KEY=sk-...
    29	OPENAI_BASE_URL=https://api.deepseek.com
    30	VOLTAGENT_MODEL=deepseek-v4-flash
    31	VOLTAGENT_MODEL_API=chat
    32	AGENT_PROFILE=office
    33	# 推荐显式工作区，避免落到默认 ~/VoltAgent-Office/workspace
    34	WORKSPACE_ROOT=/absolute/path/to/your/office-folder
    35	# VOLTAGENT_MAX_STEPS=80
    36	# VOLTAGENT_MEMORY=libsql
    37	```
    38	
    39	可选 MCP（无凭据可跳过，侧车仍可跑本地 FS）：
    40	
    41	```bash
    42	# MCP_DOCS_URL=https://your-docs-mcp.example/sse
    43	# MCP_DOCS_BEARER_TOKEN=...
    44	# MCP_CALENDAR_URL=https://your-calendar-mcp.example
    45	# 或 stdio：
    46	# MCP_DOCS_COMMAND=npx
    47	# MCP_DOCS_ARGS=-y,@your/feishu-docs-mcp
    48	```
    49	
    50	### 1.3 启动
    51	
    52	```bash
    53	# terminal 1 — sidecar :3141
    54	pnpm --filter @uilab/workbench-runtime-voltagent dev
    55	
    56	# terminal 2 — Workbench UI
    57	export VITE_RUNTIME_ADAPTER=voltagent
    58	pnpm dev:workbench
    59	# → http://localhost:5174/
    60	```
    61	
    62	日志应出现类似：
    63	
    64	```text
    65	profile=office workspaceRoot=… maxSteps=80 summarization=true memory=libsql
    66	mcp=docs=off,calendar=off
    67	note=local VoltAgent Office Runtime …; not remote production cluster
    68	```
    69	
    70	### 1.4 UI 演示路径
    71	
    72	1. 打开 Workbench → **新对话**（empty 路径，走 Runtime 而非 capture seed）。
    73	2. 确认 Timeline / Context 披露为 **本机 VoltAgent Office Runtime · 非远程生产集群**（不是 Fake）。
    74	3. **读：**「列出工作区根目录」→ 应出现 `ls` 工具行。
    75	4. **Skill：**「用会议纪要 skill 整理：……」→ 可能 `workspace_*_skill*` 后写入 `/output/meeting-notes/…`（写文件需审批）。
    76	5. **写审批：** 时间线出现审批 →「允许一次」→ 文件落在 `WORKSPACE_ROOT`。
    77	6. **取消（可选）：** 运行中点停止 → Composer 可再次发送。
    78	7. **侧车不可达：** 停掉 :3141 再发送 → 应看到可读错误，而非假成功。
    79	
    80	### 1.5 Fake / capture 回归
    81	
    82	不设 `VITE_RUNTIME_ADAPTER`（默认 fake）或打开默认 seed 任务：
    83	
    84	- capture / local-sim 仍可回放；
    85	- 文案仍为 Deterministic Fake Runtime。
    86	
    87	---
    88	
    89	## 2. Profile 与安全默认
    90	
    91	| 项 | 行为 |
    92	| --- | --- |
    93	| `AGENT_PROFILE=minimal` | DIY `read_file` / `write_file` / `run_command` |
    94	| `AGENT_PROFILE=office` | Agent + Workspace FS + Skills；可选 MCP |
    95	| 未设 `WORKSPACE_ROOT`（office） | `~/VoltAgent-Office/workspace`（首次 README） |
    96	| 写/删 FS | `needsApproval: true` |
    97	| 越界路径 | 拒绝（「路径越界」） |
    98	| MCP 写类工具 | 名称启发式 `needsApproval` |
    99	| Sandbox | 默认关 |
   100	
   101	---
   102	
   103	## 3. MCP 降级矩阵
   104	
   105	| 状态 | 日志 | 用户体验 |
   106	| --- | --- | --- |
   107	| 未配置 | `docs=off` | 仅本地 FS/Skills |
   108	| 连接成功 | `docs=ok(N)` | MCP 工具出现在 agent tools / Timeline |
   109	| 连接失败 | `docs=fail` + warn | 不崩溃；提示连接失败原因；本地仍可用 |
   110	
   111	CI **不**依赖真实飞书/日历账号；单测用 mock host。
   112	
   113	---
   114	
   115	## 4. 常用 env 速查
   116	
   117	见 `.env.example` 与 README「Profiles / O2–O5 / MCP」表。
   118	
   119	| 键 | 含义 |
   120	| --- | --- |
   121	| `AGENT_PROFILE` | `office` \| `minimal` |
   122	| `WORKSPACE_ROOT` | 授权根 |
   123	| `VOLTAGENT_MAX_STEPS` | office 默认 80 |
   124	| `VOLTAGENT_SUMMARIZATION` | office 默认 on |
   125	| `VOLTAGENT_MEMORY` | `libsql`（默认）\| `in-memory` \| `off` |
   126	| `MCP_DOCS_URL` / `MCP_CALENDAR_URL` | HTTP MCP |
   127	| `MCP_*_COMMAND` / `MCP_*_ARGS` | stdio MCP |
   128
